## R132 exact-SHA Top Smoke and Mutation replay — 2026-10-31

At unchanged source SHA `207cc3896cb635ff04e7095fc18c435080507724` (tree `caed517e162270fc2f64beb5382fecdccb651bd5`), Top Smoke passes the mapper precheck (86 descriptors, 60 tensors) and a 20-case seeded run (`AI_SEED=12345`, `TEST PASS`). The checker-sensitivity campaign passes 28/28: Address, Data, Response Owner, WSTRB, Completion Error Code, Write After Error, Response Epoch, and Done/Missing-Done/Duplicate-Done on each of seven engines all trigger their expected checker. These are monitor-side mutations and do not close the legal fault/phase/reset cross-product. ## Status summary

**R77–R82 exact-clean evidence (2026-10-30):** At code/test SHA `3fdb22640dc8b3c73e9ff05d3b07c6f0e3e67a28`, R77 real-top UVM passes three seeds with the new SFU descriptor-latch point; checker-sensitivity mutations pass 28/28; Top Smoke and 20-case seeded top pass; behavioral AXI stall/reset passes; Matrix size/ablation suites pass 45/45 and 120/120 across three seeds. Each top UVM seed checks 17,094 words with zero mismatch/unknown/stale and UVM severity `0/0/0`; focused applicable testpoint group is 31/31. R78 adds standalone arbiter fairness. At exact test SHA `a458645`, R79 checks seven production MUX clients under bounded downstream ready stalls: 3,233 accepts, max wait 10, and 2,582 owner/data/error-checked responses. At exact clean test SHA `e4c1e7a`, R80 composes the production MUX and arbiter for 4,096 cycles, accepts 4,096 requests, observes max per-engine wait 22 and verifies 3,465 read responses, ownership/error routing and descriptor priority over completion. Its three SVA and four cover properties pass. Same-SHA Top Smoke with 20 random cases passes. R81 uses this same exact code/test SHA for three-seed real-top UVM (17,094 checked words/seed), 28/28 named checker-sensitivity mutations, and five-seed Top Smoke code-coverage merge (line/condition/FSM/branch `86.38/72.92/44.59/75.36%`). R82 reruns 45 array-size and 120 ablation points at the same candidate; all 165 Golden checks pass across three seeds with deterministic cycles, traffic and stalls. R81's top UVM also records a real-top serial/staged/parallel task profile. The raw code-coverage metrics are not functional coverage; the UVM focused functional group is 31/31 applicable variables, while the 38 requirement dispositions remain 24 PASS-SCOPED / 14 PARTIAL. These bounded tests use behavioral memory and do not establish production-engine-to-SRAM fairness or complete the full shared-SPAD contract. Product RTL/SPEC remain unchanged from `9d4e9ab`; no requirement disposition changes. See [R77 replay report](COMPUTE_CLUSTER_CLEAN_REPLAY_R77_2026-10-30.md), [R78 arbiter report](COMPUTE_CLUSTER_SPAD_FAIRNESS_R78_2026-10-30.md), [R79 MUX report](COMPUTE_CLUSTER_MUX_FAIRNESS_R79_2026-10-30.md), [R80 path report](COMPUTE_CLUSTER_SPAD_PATH_FAIRNESS_R80_2026-10-30.md), [R81 audit](COMPUTE_CLUSTER_CLEAN_REPLAY_R81_2026-10-30.md), and [R82 Matrix benchmark](../benchmark/COMPUTE_CLUSTER_MATRIX_BENCHMARK_R82_2026-10-30.md).

| Disposition | Count | Meaning |
|---|---:|---|
| PASS-SCOPED | 27 | Named scenario passed; the status does not claim every legal parameter/fault cross is closed. |
| PARTIAL | 11 | Evidence exists, but one or more stated requirement clauses or legal coverage crosses remain open. |
| BLOCKED_SPEC | 0 | The v1.4 fault-priority and prefix contract removes the previous specification blocker; the associated verification requirement remains partial. |
| NOT_RUN | 0 | Each requirement has an execution disposition; blocked items are not silently called untested passes. |

### Exact-candidate clean replay record (R14)

Command, run from the detached clean candidate worktree:

```sh
make -C dv/uvm/sim uvm_compute_cluster_regress \
  COMPUTE_CLUSTER_UVM_OUT=/tmp/nmaforge_signoff_clean_c0832e5_20261027 \
  COMPUTE_CLUSTER_UVM_SEEDS='20261025 20261026 20261027'
```

The command exited `0` and printed `COMPUTE_CLUSTER_REAL_UVM_REGRESS_PASS`. Each seed reports 4,946 accepted writes, 5,256 checked words, zero mismatches/unknown reads/stale epochs/orphan responses, line WSTRB `{full:512,partial:0,zero:0}`, and drained pending ledgers. The retained UVM summary is `0/0/0`; focused group is 29/30 variables (`98.96%`).

URG assertion evidence: 22 properties, 17 successes, 5 uncovered, zero failures/incomplete; two UVM RAL properties have no attempts. The two AXI AR assertions and AW payload-stability assertion have attempts but no real successes; AW burst-bound and W payload-stability assertions each have three successes. Of the three cover properties, W stall-to-accept matched three times while AR/AW stall-to-accept did not match. URG `groups.txt` and `asserts.txt` are retained; this is a mixed testbench/UVM/DUT report, not DUT-only coverage. The report also confirms that clean replay did not change these vacuity dispositions.

## Coverage and assertion disposition

- Focused top functional coverage is 100.0% of applicable bins in current-candidate R40 across six clean seeds: URG reports 29/29 expected variables and 5/5 applicable response-client bins. The historical unadjusted score remains 29/30 (98.96%); the only omitted bin is `cp_response_client.completion`, structurally N/A because `ai_completion_writer` only issues writes. Both the UVM test and run gate require zero completion read responses, and URG exposes the ignored bin explicitly as excluded. DMA response-client traffic was observed. R267's Cluster-only `cluster_cg` reaches 64/64, while aggregate group score is 82.96%; mixed E2E separately scores 90.86% and provides AXI stall/assertion evidence. These are scoped group results, not global requirement closure. Current RTM is 27 PASS-SCOPED / 11 PARTIAL.
- Matrix default prefetch-enabled core FSM remains 15/15 states and 49/53 transitions (92.45%). `ST_MAC→ST_A_REQ` is exercised in the prefetch-disabled build R4. On the exact current candidate, R28 proves five non-vacuous properties under the documented legal response environment and bounded descriptor; both `ST_PREFETCH_WAIT` and `ST_ERROR_DRAIN` reachability covers hit. The three guard/invariant edges are formally dispositioned for this model. The raw 49/53 transition figure remains; this is not full FSM coverage across parameter profiles or integrated fault crossings. The child array remains line/condition/branch `82.35/20.00/58.82%`.
- R32 exact-candidate clean UVM compile/elaboration has zero warnings/errors and no `TFIPC`; engine `busy/done` SVA ports are connected. Its URG has 17/22 assertion properties with successes and zero failures/incomplete; three AR/AW properties are not non-vacuous in this compute-only sequence and two UVM RAL properties have no attempts. R15's AXI/FP E2E URG supplies real successes for the five AXI AR/AW/W payload/bound assertions; stall-to-accept covers match 166/70/107 times. R27 separately replays coordinated R/B reset on the current SHA with UVM severity 0/0/0. None proves every compute requester × fault/backpressure combination.
- `CLUSTER-S2-TOP-SMOKE-FA2B` retains one `[LOOP-REROLL-ENABLED]` fixture warning; it is not an SVA port warning and is not silently counted as zero-warning.
- Whole-top URG line/condition/FSM/branch/assertion/group figures include DUT, SVA, testbench and UVM. They are not DUT-only coverage. Do not use them as a cluster closure percentage.

## Sign-off interpretation

The R267/R261/R262 audit supports **CL-RC1 review** and a **bounded CL-RC2 evidence review**. It does not meet CL-DV-v1.0 closure: R99 closes `OPEN-CL-003` for the user-selected in-repository `ai_accel_memory_fabric`; broader legal fault/reset crosses and other partial requirements remain open. The 38 requirement rows are 27 PASS-SCOPED / 11 PARTIAL / 0 BLOCKED_SPEC / 0 NOT_RUN. R257 remains the latest combined URG plan at 33/33 declared groups; R267 independently replays the published candidate and reports Cluster-only `cluster_cg=64/64`, aggregate group score 82.96%, and 20/22 mixed-E2E assertions with real success. These are useful scoped evidence, not a substitute for closing the remaining clauses; the two unattempted assertions are UVM RAL internals. Matrix FSM remains 49/53 raw transitions, with R249's legal-profile/formal dispositions documented separately. R46/R47 are `BLOCKED_ENV`; R48 was a failed testbench coverage gate, corrected by R49. R55–R58 add exact-clean current-harness seven-engine UVM, checker-sensitivity, Matrix fault/recovery and prefetch-off fallback evidence; R59 replays Top Smoke and the 20-case top regression; R60 extends seeded parity to all seven engines; R64/R65 add all-engine Protection phases; R66 reconfirms 28/28 mutation catches on R65; R69 covers legal CNM local-FSM Abort/Reset phases and dispositions the core-blocked zero-length transition; R70 replays its named regressions at clean SHA `94c11ef`; R71 adds real-top Matrix Line stall stability and Top Smoke/seeded-top replay at `cfcd4db`; R73 fixes busy-time CPLQ consumer progress and passes the real-top full/hold/release/wrap test at `9d4e9ab`; R83 clean-replays accepted-read Abort/drain on every production engine; R84 clean-replays one SFU and R85 clean-replays one Conv and R86 one Matrix scalar-fallback and R88 one aligned-Line Matrix accepted-write-prefix Abort point; R87 clean-runs default-path UVM and 28/28 checker sensitivity at `402c8ce`; R88 clean-replays the aligned-Line prefix, three-seed UVM, checker sensitivity, Top Smoke and 20-case seeded Top at `ce2e36d`; R89 reruns all 8 Matrix configurations × 5 shapes with 40/40 Golden checks at the same code/test SHA. Behavioral AXI reset and selected Matrix/CNM/DMA recovery do not qualify an unidentified external fabric. See [R267 replay report](COMPUTE_CLUSTER_FINAL_CLEAN_REPLAY_R267_2026-11-03.md), [readiness report](COMPUTE_CLUSTER_DV_SIGNOFF_READINESS_2026-10-27.md), [open issue list](COMPUTE_CLUSTER_OPEN_ISSUES_2026-10-27.md).

## R73 CPLQ consumer progress and real-top backpressure — 2026-10-30

Exact clean SHA `9d4e9abdba1f745644a485960620fa6bbc4979db` reproduces a real register defect: generic `cfg_locked` returned `SLVERR` for software `CPLQ_HEAD` updates while compute was busy, so a full ordered completion queue could not recover. The minimal fix exempts only `CPLQ_HEAD` from the busy write lock; SPEC v1.8 records the existing software-consumer ownership contract. Three real-top UVM seeds fill the three usable entries of a four-slot ring with Matrix/CNM/SFU work, hold the fourth Scalar VDOT completion, advance head from 0 to 1 while busy, and verify ordered retirement and tail wrap 3→0. The queue refills, dispatcher drains, and all four output memories and completion records match independent Goldens. Each seed checks 16,286 words with zero mismatch/unknown/stale/orphan and UVM severity 0/0/0. The new `cp_cplq_full` bin hits 1/1, aggregate 20,454 hits; focused applicable compute coverage is 100.0%. Top Smoke, fixed 20-case seeded top regression and 28/28 checker-sensitivity campaign pass at the exact SHA. This strengthens REQ-PC-004 but leaves its disposition PASS-SCOPED and does not change the RTM 24/14 count or overall HOLD. See [R73 report](COMPUTE_CLUSTER_CPLQ_BACKPRESSURE_R73_2026-10-30.md).
